Using Bangladesh Demographic and Health Survey (BDHS) data collected between 2007 and 2022, we transformed maternal, child, healthcare, and household characteristics into semantically interpretable prompt-based representations and evaluated GPT-4o-mini for zero-shot stunting prediction, comparing its performance against a random forest baseline and assessing fairness across demographic and socioeconomic groups as well as temporal robustness across survey waves. The results demonstrate that zero-shot inference using GPT-4o-mini achieved comparable balanced accuracy to the supervised baseline while exhibiting substantially higher sensitivity for identifying stunting cases, relatively consistent performance across child sex groups, and stable predictive behaviour across BDHS waves; however, important fairness disparities were observed across residence and household wealth categories, highlighting the need for further investigation before deployment of foundation models in public health prediction settings.

</details>


### [105] [M3-DuplexBench: A Multi-Turn, Multilingual, Multidomain Benchmark for Full-Duplex Spoken Dialogue Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29125)
*Ryo Fukuda,Atsushi Ando,Hiroki Kanagawa,Takatomo Kano,Marc Delcroix,Naohiro Tawara,Yuya Chiba*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出M3-DuplexBench，一个支持英日双语、涵盖闲聊与多轮问答的多轮全双工对话系统基准测试，以解决现有评估在公平性、语言及领域覆盖上的不足。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 全双工 spoken dialogue systems (FDSDSs) 虽能实现自然交互，但现有基准在多轮对话公平比较、语言多样性及对话领域覆盖方面存在局限。

Method: 构建M3-DuplexBench基准，支持英语和日语，涵盖闲聊和多轮问答；评估模型在单轮、仅用户上下文及教师强制全上下文等多种对话历史设置下的表现。

Result: 实验揭示了不同模型特有的话轮转换特征，语言与领域间存在显著性能差距，且对话历史对模型行为的影响效果不一。

Conclusion: 该基准为全面评估全双工对话系统的多轮交互能力提供了新的标准，指出了当前模型在跨语言和跨领域泛化上的不足。

Abstract: Full-duplex spoken dialogue systems (FDSDSs) can listen while speaking, enabling natural behaviors such as smooth turn-taking, backchannel handling, and user barge-in handling. However, fair comparisons in multi-turn conversations remain a challenge. In addition, existing benchmarks provide limited coverage of languages and dialogue domains. We propose M3-DuplexBench, a multi-turn, multilingual, multidomain benchmark for FDSDSs. M3-DuplexBench supports English and Japanese and covers both casual conversation and multi-turn question answering. In addition, we evaluate models under multiple dialogue context settings, including single-turn, user-only, and teacher-forced full-context settings, to analyze how dialogue history affects model behavior. Experiments with recent FDSDSs reveal model-specific turn-taking characteristics, clear performance gaps across languages and domains, and mixed effects of dialogue context.

</details>


### [106] [Authorship Verification of Transcribed German-Language Videos](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29168)
*Oren Halvani,Sophie Titze*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本研究将作者验证（AV）应用于德语视频转录文本，发现基于字符和词n-gram的传统方法性能优于现代Transformer模型，证明传统方法在口语作者验证中依然有效且具竞争力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有作者验证研究主要集中于书面英语文本，缺乏对口语形式（如视频）及非英语语言（如德语）的关注，存在研究空白。

Method: 构建包含150名说话者、300个视频的德语语料库，应用10种主流作者验证方法（涵盖传统n-gram方法和现代Transformer方法）进行跨视频对的说话人身份验证实验。

Result: 基于简单字符和词n-gram表示的传统AV方法表现最佳，准确率达88%，AUC达90%；而Transformer-based方法在所有语料库上表现显著较差。

Conclusion: 在德语视频转录文本的作者验证任务中，传统方法不仅保持竞争力，而且仍然相关，证明了其在处理口语文本时的有效性。

Abstract: Authorship Verification (AV) represents an important subfield of digital text forensics and addresses the fundamental question of whether two texts were written by the same author. Although the field has made substantial progress over the past two decades, several important challenges remain unresolved or underexplored. For instance, most AV research has focused on written texts, despite the fact that language is expressed not only in written but also in spoken form, such as in videos. Moreover, existing AV studies have predominantly concentrated on English, while other languages, including German, have received comparatively little attention. To address these research gaps, we apply AV to spoken language in the form of transcripts of German-language videos and examine the effectiveness of established AV methods in verifying a speaker's identity across video pairs. Our experimental evaluation, based on a total of ten AV methods applied to three self-compiled corpora comprising 300 videos from 150 speakers, shows that the best performance (up to 88% accuracy and 90% AUC) is achieved by traditional AV approaches based on simple character- and token n-gram representations. In contrast, more modern transformer-based approaches perform significantly worse on all evaluated corpora. Our results therefore suggest that traditional methods in the field of AV remain both competitive and relevant.

</details>


### [107] [Learning Latent Reasoning Traces for Scalar Reward Models End-to-End](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29185)
*Sanwoo Lee,Clive Bai,Hsiu-Yuan Huang,Kun Liang,Weijie Liu,Yunfang Wu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出LatentRM框架，通过将对角化推理轨迹作为离散潜变量，显式最大化下游标量奖励的似然，解决了传统标量奖励模型泛化性差与生成式奖励模型缺乏数值灵活性之间的矛盾，在偏好建模和策略对齐任务上优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统标量奖励模型（RMs）依赖表面线索，难以泛化到复杂或分布外（OOD）任务；生成式奖励模型虽鲁棒性强，但自然语言分数缺乏数值灵活性和概率可解释性。现有的混合方法通过并行优化未能保证生成的推理轨迹能有效促进下游标量奖励预测。

Method: 提出LatentRM框架，将中间推理轨迹学习为离散潜变量，并通过端到端的在线策略优化（on-policy optimization）紧密耦合基于深度推理的评价与精确评分，显式最大化下游标量奖励的似然。

Result: 在分布内和分布外数据集以及RLHF验证中，LatentRM在从开放式对话到复杂推理的各种任务的偏好建模和策略对齐方面，均优于标量、生成式和混合奖励模型。

Conclusion: LatentRM通过显式对齐推理过程与标量奖励，成功结合了生成式模型的鲁棒性和标量模型的可解释性与灵活性，显著提升了大语言模型与人类偏好对齐的效果。

Abstract: Reward models (RMs) are central to aligning large language models with human preferences via reinforcement learning. Although traditional scalar RMs enable efficient and probabilistic reward modeling, they rely on superficial cues that fail to generalize to complex or out-of-distribution (OOD) tasks. Conversely, generative RMs leverage extensive reasoning to improve robustness on challenging tasks, but their natural language-based scores lack the numerical flexibility and probabilistic interpretability that scalar RMs offer. While recent approaches combine both paradigms through off-policy multi-task learning, such parallel optimization does not guarantee that generated reasoning traces actively align with or benefit downstream scalar reward prediction. To address this mismatch, we propose LatentRM, a reward modeling framework that learns intermediate reasoning traces as discrete latent variables to explicitly maximize the likelihood of downstream scalar rewards. Through on-policy optimization of the latent reasoning space end-to-end, LatentRM tightly couples deep reasoning-based evaluation with precise scoring. Extensive validations on in-distribution and OOD datasets and RLHF show that LatentRM outperforms scalar, generative, and hybrid RMs on preference modeling and policy alignment across tasks ranging from open-ended conversation to complex reasoning.

</details>


### [108] [Detecting Experiential Intertextuality Across Migration Routes: Beyond Surface Similarity in French Narratives](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29188)
*Sakayo Toadoum Sari,Nelly Robin,Michelle Auzanneau,Lakhdar Sais,Veronique Petit,Marie Veniard,Said Jabbour,Fabien Delorme*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 研究提出了“体验互文性检测”任务，旨在无监督识别跨迁移路线叙事中的共同经历。通过对比多种基线、嵌入方法及大语言模型（LLM），发现结合31个特征的监督混合模型效果最佳（相关系数r=0.45），显著优于单一方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 迁移者穿越不同地理路线（如跨撒哈拉和巴尔干走廊）时往往有相似的经历（警察暴力、走私剥削等）。现有方法缺乏无需标注数据即可自动识别这些叙事中共享体验回声的能力。

Method: 从108篇法语迁移叙事中生成句子对，使用以下无标注评分方法：词汇基线、句子嵌入、基于词性的结构特征、迁移主题词典、上下文感知叙事特征，以及Qwen2.5-7B和Mistral-7B的零样本/少样本LLM评分。最终构建一个结合所有31个特征的监督混合模型，并与816条专家标注数据进行验证。

Result: 1. 表面、结构和嵌入方法与专家判断的相关性较弱（r≤0.30）；2. Qwen2.5-7B零样本表现最好（r=0.38）；3. 少样本示例降低Qwen性能但显著提升Mistral；4. 叙事位置具有预测性，出发阶段配对体验回声最高；5. 监督混合模型达到r=0.45，比最佳单一方法提升21%。

Conclusion: 简单的文本匹配或嵌入方法难以有效捕捉迁移叙事中的深层体验互文性。结合语义、结构和上下文的多特征监督学习能显著提升检测精度，其中LLM在特定策略下表现突出，且叙事阶段对体验共鸣有重要影响。

Abstract: Migrants traversing geographically distinct routes such as the Trans-Saharan and Balkan corridors often recount strikingly parallel lived experiences: police violence, smuggler exploitation, dangerous crossings, and family separation. We introduce the task of experiential intertextuality detection: automatically identifying shared experiential echoes across migration narratives without requiring annotated training data. From 108 French migration narratives spanning both corridors, we automatically generate sentence pairs and score them using annotation-free methods: lexical baselines, sentence embeddings, POS-based structural features, a migration-specific theme lexicon, context-aware narrative features, and zero-shot LLM scoring with Qwen2.5-7B and Mistral-7B under three prompting strategies. We validate all methods against 816 expert-annotated intertextuality judgments (inter-annotator Krippendorff's $α= 0.27$). Our results reveal that all surface, structural, and embedding methods correlate only weakly with expert judgments ($r \leq 0.30$); Qwen2.5-7B zero-shot achieves the best single-method correlation ($r = 0.38$); few-shot examples degrade Qwen but dramatically improve Mistral; narrative position significantly predicts intertextuality, with departure-phase pairs showing the highest experiential echoes; and a supervised hybrid combining all 31 features achieves $r = 0.45$, a 21% improvement over the best individual method.

</details>


### [109] [Hy-MultiTurn: A Six-Dimensional Benchmark for Deep Multi-Turn Dialogue Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29196)
*Eileen Ye,Jiawen Tao,Yaoming Li,Chenxu Liu,Wenhan Yu,Yaxin Fan,Xiaokun Yuan,Mengzhou Wu,Yanbing Jiang,Maxm Pan*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出中文深度多轮对话基准Hy-MultiTurn，通过六种受控评估模式解决现有长程交互评测不足的问题，测试模型在约束记忆、精确执行等六方面的能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准多为短对话且缺乏对长程交互中记忆、追踪修订及指代解析等能力的全面评估，尤其在中国语言场景下，且难以深入分析模型失败原因。

Method: 分析真实聊天机器人故障机制，定义六种受控评估模式（约束记忆、精确执行、约束合成、对象定位、动作抑制、指代解析），构建包含209个任务、跨度12-76轮的Hy-MultiTurn基准，并加入无关话题干扰和口语化表达以增加难度。

Result: 评估22种前沿模型配置显示该基准具有广泛挑战性；最强的GPT-5.5仅在41.1%的响应中满足所有要求，且没有模型在所有六种模式下表现最佳。

Conclusion: Hy-MultiTurn能有效揭示当前大模型在深层多轮对话理解中的局限性，为后续研究提供了更具挑战性和诊断性的评估工具。

Abstract: Long-running multi-turn interactions with chatbots and agents are now common, and a correct response often depends on remembering earlier details, tracking later revisions, identifying intended objects or referents, and withholding action when required conditions are unmet. Existing multi-turn benchmarks typically cover short exchanges and do not fully evaluate these capabilities in long multi-turn interactions, particularly in Chinese, while offering limited insight into how and why models fail. To address these limitations, we analyze real chatbot failures to identify six recurring mechanisms and use them to define six controlled evaluation modes in Hy-MultiTurn, a Chinese benchmark for deep multi-turn dialogue understanding. The six modes evaluate constraint memory, precise execution, constraint synthesis, object localization, action suppression, and reference resolution. Across the six modes, we construct 209 controlled tasks spanning 12-76 turns, with dialogue length, irrelevant-topic distraction, and colloquial phrasing adding further difficulty. Evaluation of 22 frontier model configurations shows that Hy-MultiTurn is broadly challenging, as even GPT-5.5, the strongest overall configuration, satisfies all requirements in only 41.1 percent of responses and no model performs best in all six modes.

</details>


### [110] [Knowing When to Quit: Diagnosing and Training LLMs to Abort Futile Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29211)
*Xinyan Guan,Jiali Zeng,Chunlei Xin,Yaojie Lu,Hongyu Lin,Xianpei Han,Le Sun,Fandong Meng*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 针对大语言模型在超出能力任务上产生“徒劳推理”（看似合理但错误）的问题，提出CaRL方法。通过奖励塑造和回溯拒绝增强，引导模型在无法胜任时选择拒绝而非强行生成错误答案，从而在保持性能的同时显著减少误导性输出。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型在处理超出其能力的任务时，常生成计算成本高但语义空洞的推理过程。这种“徒劳推理”现象表现为模型过度自信地给出表面合理实则错误的推导，极易误导用户，造成潜在风险。现有研究缺乏对这一现象的系统性刻画及有效抑制机制。

Method: 提出**CaRL**（Capability-aligned Reinforcement Learning，能力对齐强化学习）。该方法包含两个核心组件：1) **奖励塑造**：通过设计奖励函数，激励模型在面临困难任务时选择“拒绝回答”，而非进行徒劳推理；2) **回溯拒绝增强**：将模型失败的历史数据转化为拒绝行为的监督信号，进一步优化模型识别自身能力边界的能力。

Result: 实验表明，CaRL显著减少了模型在各类难度任务上的徒劳推理行为，同时保持了原有的任务性能。模型能够更准确地判断何时应停止推理并拒绝回答，实现了行为与能力边界的对齐，且未牺牲实用性。

Conclusion: 通过强化学习使模型行为与其实际能力边界对齐，是解决大模型“幻觉式”推理和误导性输出的有效途径。CaRL证明了在不降低任务表现的前提下，让模型学会“知难而退”（即拒绝回答）可显著提升输出的可靠性和安全性。

Abstract: Large language models generate computationally expensive yet semantically void reasoning on beyond-capability tasks, creating risks where plausible-sounding but incorrect derivations mislead users. We characterize this \textit{futile reasoning} phenomenon through systematic analysis, revealing universal capability overreach and systematic miscalibration between capability and behavior. The dominant failure mode is specious reasoning, which outputs look superficially valid but contain subtle errors, escalating with task difficulty. To address this, we introduce \textbf{CaRL} (\textbf{Ca}pability-\textbf{a}ligned \textbf{R}einforcement \textbf{L}earning), which aligns model behavior with capability boundaries through reward shaping that incentivizes refusal over futile reasoning and hindsight refusal augmentation that converts failures into refusal supervision. Experiments demonstrate a substantial reduction in futile reasoning while preserving performance across task difficulties, effectively achieving capability-aligned behavior without sacrificing utility. \footnote{https://github.com/icip-cas/Knowing-When-to-Quit}

</details>


### [111] [Small Is Enough: Per-User Style Rewriting of AI-Edited Text via LoRA Adapters](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29238)
*Antorweep Chakravorty*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: InMyStyle是一个隐私优先的单用户系统，利用小型语言模型（0.5B-7B）和LoRA微调技术，无需推理指令即可将AI编辑的文本重写为特定用户的写作风格。该系统通过本地辅助LLM构建训练数据，在科学论文语料库上自动综合得分达到0.69，且小模型已足以胜任该任务，模型大小主要影响效率而非质量上限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在解决AI生成或编辑文本缺乏个性化风格的问题，同时强调隐私保护和降低“AI感”，避免依赖大型云端模型带来的隐私泄露风险和高昂的计算成本。

Method: 1. 使用多个本地辅助LLM根据用户文档构建配对训练样本；2. 对0.5B至7B参数量的基础模型进行LoRA适配器微调；3. 采用长度感知生成预算和自动分块技术以支持不同长度的输入；4. 在推理阶段无需指令提示直接进行风格重写。

Result: 1. 在219个科学论文评估对中，自动综合评分在贪婪解码和采样解码下均稳定在0.69（满分1），表明小模型足以完成该重写任务；2. 400次由5个LLM法官进行的评估显示，InMyStyle输出的平均“AI感”评分比原始AI输入低20%以上；3. InMyStyle内部，随着模型尺寸减小，感知到的AI感进一步降低。

Conclusion: 小型语言模型配合LoRA微调技术在个性化文本重写任务中表现充足，模型规模主要决定资源与性能的权衡，而非质量的绝对排名。该方法能有效降低输出文本的AI痕迹，同时保障用户隐私。

Abstract: InMyStyle is a privacy first, single user system that adapts small language models to rewrite AI-edited text towards an individual user's writing style without an instruction prompt at inference. Given a user's documents, it uses multiple local helper LLMs to construct paired training examples and fine tunes LoRA adapters on base models ranging from 0.5B to 7B parameters. Length aware generation budgets and automatic chunking support inputs of different lengths. On 219 evaluation pairs from a scientific-paper corpus, the automatic composite score plateaus at 0.69 [scale 0-1] across all model sizes under both greedy and sampled decoding. This observed plateau suggests that small models are sufficient for the measured rewriting task, with model size determining trade-offs rather than a stable quality ranking. As a secondary evaluation, 400 ratings from five LLM judges give InMyStyle outputs a mean perceived AI-ness score over 20% lower than their helper-AI generated inputs, while mean perceived AI-ness scores decrease with model size within InMyStyle.

</details>


### [112] [Data Turnstile: A Scalable Open Framework for Function-Calling Data Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29250)
*Goutham Ramakrishnan,Megha Sharma*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出Data Turnstile框架，通过生成高质量合成数据解决小语言模型（SLMs）在工具使用任务中因数据稀缺和噪声导致的效果瓶颈。该框架利用API规范进行分步生成与验证，显著提升了Qwen3系列小模型在函数调用基准测试中的性能，使其接近甚至超越参数量大得多的基座模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 小语言模型（SLMs）虽具备低延迟、低成本和隐私优势，但在工具使用任务中表现不佳，主要原因是训练数据稀缺且噪声大。由于SLM容量有限，无法像大模型那样通过参数规模弥补低质量监督带来的不足，因此数据质量成为其关键瓶颈。

Method: 提出Data Turnstile开源框架，基于用户定义的API规范生成高质量的函数调用合成训练数据。该方法将多轮工具交互分解为受限的分步生成过程，并结合验证和错误反馈循环，实现对API多样性、对话复杂度和输出正确性的细粒度控制。

Result: 在BFCL单轮基准上，未经思维链（CoT）微调的Qwen3-0.6B准确率从67.4%提升至75.9%，缩小了与更大模型的差距；在$τ^2$-bench多轮基准上，Qwen3-1.7B在电信领域通过率提升4.7倍至31.1%，超越参数量大19倍的Qwen2.5-32B-Instruct；Qwen3-0.6B也提升7倍至24.6%，逼近大模型水平。同时发布了包含1000+ API和10万+多轮交互的数据集。

Conclusion: 数据质量是提升小语言模型工具使用能力的关键。Data Turnstile通过高质量合成数据生成，有效解决了SLMs在功能调用任务中的性能短板，证明了通过改进数据而非单纯增加模型规模，也能显著提升小模型在复杂代理任务中的竞争力。

Abstract: Small language models (SLMs) are attractive for agentic deployment due to low latency, reduced cost, and on-device privacy, yet they struggle with tool-use tasks where training data is scarce and noisy. Unlike larger models, SLMs cannot compensate for low-quality supervision through sheer capacity, making data quality the critical bottleneck. We present Data Turnstile, an open-source framework that takes user-defined API specifications and generates high-quality synthetic training data for function calling. Turnstile decomposes multi-turn tool-use interactions into constrained, stepwise generation with validation and error-feedback loops, providing fine-grained control over API diversity, conversation complexity, and output correctness. We demonstrate effectiveness of domain adaptation with Turnstile data on two challenging function calling benchmarks. On the BFCL single-turn benchmark, a Qwen3-0.6B fine-tuned on Turnstile data without chain-of-thought achieves 75.9% overall accuracy (versus 67.4% for the base model with thinking enabled), closing the gap with thinking-enabled Qwen3-1.7B (78.4%) and Qwen3-4B (79.9%) despite being 3$\times$ and 7$\times$ smaller respectively. On $τ^2$-bench, a multi-turn agentic benchmark, Turnstile-trained Qwen3-1.7B achieves 31.1% pass^1 on the Telecom domain, improving 4.7$\times$ over its 6.6% base and surpassing Qwen2.5-32B-Instruct (27.4%), a model 19$\times$ larger. Turnstile-trained Qwen3-0.6B achieves 24.6%, improving 7$\times$ over its 3.5% base and approaching the 32B model (53$\times$ larger). We release Data Turnstile along with a dataset spanning 1,000+ APIs and 100K+ multi-turn interactions.

</details>


### [113] [CalibratedRubric: Task-Adaptive Rubric Banks for Open-Ended LLM Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29252)
*Mengting Chen,Yanshu Sun,Wanting Liang,Beidi Luan,Rui Sun,Dezhi Chen,Jing Li,Zuo Bai*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出CalibratedRubric框架，通过贝叶斯可测性过滤和IRT库组装，解决开放域LLM输出评估中专家评分成本高且现有自动方法区分力不足的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自动化评估管道依赖严格的裁判一致性和二元方差过滤器，无法有效区分“可测量”的评分标准与“信息丰富”的标准，且专家制定细粒度评分标准成本高昂、难以扩展。

Method: 引入CalibratedRubric任务自适应框架，结合类型特定评分、基于Beta-Bernoulli同意后验的贝叶斯评分标准可测性过滤，以及基于项目反应理论（IRT）的子模态信息覆盖目标进行紧凑评分标准库构建。

Result: 在金融、医疗、通用和法律基准测试中，可测性过滤将JudgmentBench上的人类-黄金标准一致性从κ=0.604提升至0.743；IRT贪婪选择在FinResearchBench决策支持任务中仅需49个而非131个评分标准即可达到目标相关性，并提升了跨拟合秩保真度。

Conclusion: CalibratedRubric是一种高效、感知不确定性的开放域LLM评估方法，其校准收益取决于足够的裁判冗余，任务标签扰动结果进一步证实了任务自适应评分的实际相关性。

Abstract: Reliable evaluation of open-ended LLM outputs requires fine-grained rubrics, yet expert curation is costly and difficult to scale. Existing automated pipelines rely on strict judge unanimity and binary variance filters, which cannot distinguish measurable rubrics from informative ones. We introduce CalibratedRubric, a task-adaptive framework that combines type-specific scoring, Bayesian rubric-measurability filtering, and item response theory (IRT)-based bank assembly. CalibratedRubric estimates each rubric's measurability with a Beta--Bernoulli agreement posterior and uses a submodular information-coverage objective to construct compact rubric banks over the observed capability range. Across financial, healthcare, general, and legal benchmarks, measurability filtering improves human-gold agreement on JudgmentBench from $κ=0.604$ to $0.743$. IRT-based greedy selection improves cross-fitted rank fidelity over random selection across all six evaluated response blocks and requires only 49 rather than 131 rubrics to reach the target correlation on FinResearchBench decision-support tasks. Task-label perturbations further reduce system separation, confirming the practical relevance of task-adaptive scoring. These results support CalibratedRubric as an efficient, uncertainty-aware approach to open-ended LLM evaluation, with calibration gains depending on sufficient judge redundancy.

</details>


### [114] [Translation with Thought: Difficulty-Adaptive Reasoning via Reinforcement Learning for Multi-Domain Machine Translation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29287)
*Yongshi Ye,Biao Fu,Chongxuan Huang,Yidong Chen,Xiaodong Shi*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出TwT框架，通过模拟人类直觉与深思的推理切换机制，在多域机器翻译中实现高质量、低Token消耗的高效翻译。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多域机器翻译面临不同领域语言复杂性差异巨大的挑战；受人类译者根据难度调整推理努力程度的启发，旨在解决资源效率与翻译质量之间的平衡问题。

Method: 提出“带思维的翻译”（TwT）资源理性框架：1. 监督微调：使用从DeepSeek-R1蒸馏并经GPT-4o重写以体现类人推理经济性的困难感知长思维链数据进行训练；2. 强化学习：采用混合奖励函数优化翻译质量和推理效率。

Result: 在15个基准测试（涵盖域内/外设置及3种已见/59种未见语言）上评估，TwT-7B和TwT-14B模型在翻译质量上优于更大的SOTA推理模型，同时将Token使用量减少32%–60%。

Conclusion: 将翻译行为与认知原则对齐，能够促进鲁棒的泛化能力、高翻译质量以及高效的推理过程。

Abstract: Multi-domain machine translation (MDMT) poses a unique challenge due to varying levels of linguistic complexity across domains. Inspired by human translators' ability to adapt reasoning effort based on difficulty, we propose TwT (Translation with Thought), a resource-rational framework that learns to modulate inference between intuitive and deliberate reasoning. TwT is trained in two stages: (1) supervised fine-tuning on difficulty-aware long chain-of-thought traces distilled from DeepSeek-R1 and rewritten by GPT-4o to reflect human-like reasoning economy, and (2) reinforcement learning with a hybrid reward to optimize translation quality and reasoning efficiency. Evaluated on 15 benchmarks spanning in-domain and out-of-domain settings, as well as 3 seen and 59 unseen languages, with ablations across three backbone models, TwT-7B and TwT-14B outperform much larger SOTA reasoning models in translation quality, while reducing token usage by 32--60\%. These results confirm that aligning translation behavior with cognitive principles enables robust generalization, high translation quality, and efficient reasoning in MDMT.

</details>


### [115] [Cross-Lingual Transfer for Machine Translation in Turkic Languages](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29355)
*Omer Burak Cinar,Mehmet Mert Dalkilic,Cagri Toraman*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 研究低资源机器翻译中，同属突厥语族的五种语言（土耳其语、阿塞拜疆语、乌兹别克语、哈萨克语、吉尔吉斯语）之间的跨语言迁移行为。发现紧密相关的语言对迁移效果最强，且迁移方向和目标语言会影响结果；拉丁化能提升部分指标但效果不均，迁移源在不同设置下具有稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管跨语言迁移对低资源机器翻译至关重要，但其在密切相关的语言家族内部的行为特征尚不充分明确，特别是缺乏对具体语言对迁移模式的细致刻画。

Method: 使用成对迁移矩阵，在mT5模型上对五种突厥语进行实验。每个模型使用一种转移源语言进行微调，并在不同的转移目标语言上进行评估，同时保持翻译目标一致，以分析不同语言对之间的迁移效果及脚本差异的影响。

Result: 1. 紧密相关的突厥语对之间迁移效果最强，尤其是土耳其语-阿塞拜疆语和哈萨克语-吉尔吉斯语；2. 迁移方向影响显著，同一源-目标对在翻译目标变化时表现不同；3. 拉丁化在多种脚本不匹配设置中提升了BLEU和chrF分数，但对不同指标的效果不一致；4. 迁移源在不同数据集和模型设置下表现出较高的稳定性。

Conclusion: 在密切相关语言家族内部，语言亲缘关系显著影响跨语言迁移效率，且迁移行为受方向和上下文制约。该研究为理解低资源场景下的多语言迁移机制提供了实证依据，并指出脚本标准化（如拉丁化）的潜在价值及其局限性。

Abstract: Cross-lingual transfer is central to low-resource machine translation, but its behavior within closely related language families remains insufficiently characterized. We study transfer among five Turkic languages; Turkish, Azerbaijani, Uzbek, Kazakh, and Kyrgyz; using pairwise transfer matrices. In this setting, each model is fine-tuned with one transfer source and evaluated on a different transfer target while the translation target remains the same. Across mT5 experiments, we find that transfer is strongest between closely related Turkic pairs, especially Turkish-Azerbaijani and Kazakh-Kyrgyz. We also show that transfer direction matters, and that the same transfer source-transfer target pair can behave differently when the translation target changes. Latinization improves BLEU and chrF in several script-mismatched settings, but its effect is not uniform across metrics. Additional analyses show that transfer sources are mostly stable across different datasets and model settings.

</details>


### [116] [Zero-Mem: Zero-Token Memory Operations for LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29377)
*Yilin Xiao,Zhehan Zhu,Yujing Zhang,Jin Chen,Zijin Hong,Luyao Zhuang,Qinggang Zhang,Shengyuan Chen,Xiaocao Ouyang,Lingfei Ren,Xiao Huang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出Zero-Mem，一种无需LLM调用即可实现结构化记忆的代理记忆系统。通过实体-上下文图和时序层次结构组织原始交互记录，仅在最终问答阶段使用LLM，显著降低时间和token成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM代理在长期交互中依赖额外LLM调用来操作记忆，导致高昂的token和时间成本，且可能因细节遗漏或合并而模糊原始证据。旨在探索是否必须通过生成中间表示来实现结构化记忆访问。

Method: 引入“零token记忆操作”，不消耗LLM输入/输出token进行记忆管理。将原始交互痕迹组织为两种互补视图：1）实体-上下文图（暴露跨交互连接）；2）时序层次结构（保留对话局部性和会话状态）。查询时加权检索两者，利用其结构恢复支持关系或上下文，并通过确定性校准剔除冲突证据，仅最终问答阶段调用LLM。

Result: 在长记忆和长上下文问答基准测试中达到竞争性性能。与最快基线相比，在相同最终QA读者和上下文预算下，将记忆操作时间成本降低57.6%。消融实验验证了双视图及其查询依赖协调的贡献。

Conclusion: 结构化代理记忆无需生成过去的中间表示即可有效运作。Zero-Mem证明了通过直接组织和检索原始交互痕迹，可消除记忆操作中的LLM调用开销，同时保持高性能。

Abstract: LLM agents need memory to act consistently over long interactions, yet many systems use additional LLM calls to operate that memory. Generating intermediate records and mediating their retrieval adds recurring token and time costs, while omitted or merged details can obscure the original evidence. We ask whether structured memory access requires generation at all. Zero-Mem introduces \emph{zero-token memory operations}: no step outside final question answering invokes an LLM or consumes LLM input or output tokens; encoder computation is accounted for separately. Zero-Mem preserves original interaction traces as its source of record. It organizes the traces in two complementary ways. An entity--context graph exposes connections across interactions, while a temporal hierarchy preserves conversational locality and session state. For each query, Zero-Mem weighs the two views, retrieves from both, and follows their structure to recover supporting relations or surrounding context. Deterministic calibration first discards conflicting evidence and then keeps the reader's answer grounded in the retrieved traces. Only the final-QA reader invokes an LLM. Across long-memory and long-context question-answering benchmarks, Zero-Mem achieves competitive performance while eliminating LLM calls and LLM-token consumption from memory operations. With the same final-QA reader and context budget, it reduces memory-operation time cost by 57.6\% relative to the fastest compared baseline. Ablations support the contribution of the two views and their query-dependent coordination. Overall, the results show that structured agent memory need not generate an intermediate representation of the past. After peer review, the code and implementation details will be available at \textcolor{blue}{https://github.com/TheMoon0815/Zero-mem}.

</details>


### [117] [PTP: Previous-Token Prediction based LLM Inversion for Near-Exact Prompt Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29378)
*Pirzada Suhail,Nagasai Saketh Naidu,Atanu R Sinha,Amit Sethi*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出一种黑盒逆向方法，通过从零训练一个显式的逆语言模型（Inverse LM），利用目标LLM生成的合成数据进行“前向词预测”的反向训练，实现无需辅助工具或模型权重的提示词重构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM逆向工作多依赖微调预训练序列到序列模型、外部大型数据集以及访问模型权重或logits，限制了其在黑盒场景下的应用；且存在提示与响应映射复杂的问题，亟需一种更通用、无需额外依赖的逆向重建方案。

Method: 1. 在黑盒设置下，从目标LLM自身生成合成数据；2. 从零开始训练一个显式的逆语言模型；3. 采用“前向词预测”机制进行训练，建立与前向自回归过程的生成性联系；4. 支持通过采样生成多种语义合理但不同的提示词，这些提示词在目标LLM中均能产生相似响应。

Result: 1. 该方法跨数据集具有泛化能力，并能迁移用于重构由不同LLM生成的响应中的提示词；2. 在基于token的提示词和响应重构评估指标上，优于先前工作。

Conclusion: 所提出的函数式逆向方法无需辅助手段即可实现高保真度的提示词重构，具备良好的泛化性和迁移性，为LLM逆向工程提供了一种更有效、更实用的黑盒解决方案。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) generate text by auto-regressively sampling the next token. This inherently leads to a many-to-many mapping between prompts and responses, complicating the task of inferring prompts from observed outputs. Prior work on LLM inversion frames prompt recovery as a semantic reconstruction task. They rely on fine-tuning pretrained sequence-to-sequence models on large external datasets--and requiring access to model weights or logits--to generate semantically plausible prompts. In contrast, we present a functional approach to inverting a given LLM in a black-box setting, without auxiliary aids. We train an explicit inverse language model entirely from scratch on data synthetically generated from the target LLM itself. Analogous to forward next-token prediction, our inverse model is trained using previous-token prediction, establishing a generative link between the forward and inverse processes that enables faithful prompt reconstruction. Moreover, it naturally supports diverse prompt reconstructions through sampling, whereby all such prompts induce similar responses under the forward, target LLM. Our approach generalises across datasets and exhibits transferability in reconstructing prompts from responses generated by different LLMs. Further, across the set of token based evaluation metrics for prompt and response reconstructions, our approach outperforms prior work.

</details>


### [118] [Studying quantization trade-offs for efficient inference deployment in machine translation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29397)
*Jim Zhao,Sohir Maskey,Koen Oostermeijer,Douglas Orr,Teryn Jones*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 研究在单GPU上部署大型语言模型进行机器翻译时，量化与文档分块策略对延迟、吞吐量和翻译质量的影响。发现结合文档分块与W4A8/W8A8量化可优化性能，但不同模型对量化的敏感度差异巨大，且标准句级评估无法准确预测长上下文下的量化影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究很少在受控的编排级工作负载下评估量化对延迟和吞吐量的实际影响；同时，传统机器翻译基准测试依赖孤立句子，无法捕捉长上下文动态及其与量化的交互作用。

Method: 1. 选取EuroLLM和Hy-MT2两个翻译模型家族（1.7B至22B参数），在单张A100或H100 GPU上进行实验。
2. 结合文档分块策略与W4A8或W8A8量化技术，评估其在不同工作负载下的延迟-吞吐量帕累托曲线。
3. 引入基于WMT24++的文档级评估方法，对比标准句级评估，分析文本分块策略在量化环境下对翻译质量的影响。

Result: 1. 文档分块结合W4A8/W8A8量化显著改善了多种工作负载下的延迟-吞吐量平衡。
2. 标准句级评估无法有效预测量化与长上下文文档翻译之间的交互作用。
3. Hy-MT2模型在量化下保持鲁棒性，而EuroLLM对量化高度敏感，所有量化格式均导致其翻译质量迅速崩溃。

Conclusion: 推理效率与翻译质量之间的权衡不仅取决于量化格式的选择，还强烈依赖于文本分块策略及模型本身的架构特性。在进行高效部署时，需综合考虑量化、分块策略及模型敏感性，并采用文档级评估而非仅依赖句级基准。

Abstract: Deploying large language models in realistic server environments poses challenges, as the system needs to provide high-quality responses with low latency. Quantization is a common approach to reduce the memory footprint and improve inference efficiency, yet its impact on latency and throughput is rarely evaluated under controlled, orchestration-level workloads. In this work we study the quantization trade-offs of two translation model families, EuroLLM \citep{martins2025eurollm} and Hy-MT2 \citep{zheng2026hy} across five models ranging from 1.7B to 22B for efficient deployment on a single A100 or H100 GPU. We demonstrate that combining a document-chunking strategy with W4A8 or W8A8 quantization improves the latency-throughput Pareto-curve under a wide range of workloads. Furthermore, since standard machine translation (MT) benchmarks rely on isolated sentences and fail to capture long-context dynamics, we introduce a document-level evaluation from WMT24++ to assess how text chunking strategies affect translation quality under quantization. Our results reveal that standard segment-level evaluation can fail to predict the interaction between quantization and long-context document translation. While Hy-MT2 remains robust under quantization, EuroLLM shows strong sensitivity and translation quality collapses rapidly for all considered quantization formats. Overall, our experiments show that the trade-off between inference efficiency and translation quality depends not only on the quantization format, but also on the choice of text chunking strategy.

</details>


### [119] [Know It, Act on It: Investigating Memory Utilization in LLM Personalization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29433)
*Zhaoxin Feng,Jianfei Ma,Emmanuele Chersoni*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 研究揭示了LLM智能体在记忆利用上的“知行脱节”现象：模型能记住用户偏好（Know），但在实际交互中未能据此调整行为（Act）。通过解耦评估范式发现，尽管记忆架构有所改善，但在健康和治疗等高风险场景下，这种利用失败依然严重。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着LLM智能体向个性化伴侣发展，记忆成为核心能力。然而存在知识利用难题：即使上下文包含相关用户偏好，模型也可能无法据此行动。现有问题在于难以区分是“遗忘”还是“记住了但未使用”，因此需要一种方法来隔离并量化这一断裂点。

Method: 引入解耦评估范式（decoupled evaluation paradigm），对同一用户偏好分别进行“知晓测试”（Know test）和“行动测试”（Act test）。在16个系统和5种记忆架构上，针对嵌入三种表达强度水平的1,000个用户偏好进行了大规模实验。

Result: Know和Act结果之间存在巨大差距：智能体通常能通过回忆测试，但在配对的行为场景中却未能体现该偏好。虽然不同的记忆架构有助于缩小这一差距，但在健康和治疗相关的偏好上，利用能力依然薄弱，而这些领域的行动失败具有最高的现实风险。

Conclusion: LLM智能体面临严重的知识利用瓶颈，即“知而不行”。未来的改进需重点关注如何将记忆转化为实际的行为调整，特别是在高 stakes 领域如心理健康护理中，当前的记忆架构尚不足以支持可靠的个性化响应。

Abstract: As large language model (LLM) agents evolve into personalized companions, memory has emerged as a core capability. However, LLMs face a knowledge utilization problem: they may fail to act on relevant user preferences even when they are fully present in context. When an agent fails to tailor its response in a context where previously shared user preferences should matter, it is unclear whether the model failed to remember that information or remembered it but failed to use it. To isolate this breakdown, we introduce a decoupled evaluation paradigm that administers paired Know and Act tests to the same user preference. We conduct large-scale experiments across 16 systems and five memory architectures, evaluating 1,000 preferences embedded at three levels of expression strength. Our results show a large gap between Know and Act outcomes: agents often pass the recall test for a user preference but fail to reflect that same preference in the paired behavioral scenario. While memory architectures reduce this gap, utilization remains especially weak for health and therapy-related preferences, where failures to act carry the greatest real-world stakes.

</details>


### [120] [Evidence-Type Competition: When Can Interventional Data Teach Language Models Causal Direction?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29484)
*Xining Xun*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 研究表明，干预数据并非因果推理的唯一金标准；模型在预训练中学到的因果能力会被推理时的观测上下文抑制。通过擦除上下文中的观测证据或仅使用干预探针，可显著恢复模型的因果方向判断准确率，且该机制由上下文内容而非训练混合比例决定。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 验证“干预数据是教导模型因果推理的金标准”这一假设，特别是在观察性相关性与因果效应符号相反（辛普森悖论情境）下的表现。

Method: 在完全受控的合成环境中，对比不同训练数据混合比例及推理时上下文类型（纯观测、混合、纯干预）对模型因果方向判断的影响；使用激活修补（activation patching）定位关键层；进行外部审计分析先验结构。

Result: 1. 增加预训练中干预样本比例并不能改善因果方向判断，模型会复制上下文的符号。2. 推理时的上下文类型决定干预证据的使用：纯观测上下文导致系统性符号反转（29/50世界），混合上下文较少（19/50），纯干预探针正确率高（41/50）。3. 擦除上下文中的观测证据可释放被抑制的因果插值能力。4. 该抑制效应在不同训练种子和参数规模下稳定存在。5. 激活修补显示开关位于中间层的观测行。6. 提出的证据平均协议将符号错误率从26%降至9%。

Conclusion: 模型具备因果推理的能力存在于权重中，但能否激活取决于推理时的上下文内容。上下文中的观测证据会抑制因果推断，而移除该抑制或提供一致的干预上下文即可恢复准确判断。这挑战了仅靠增加干预训练数据即可提升因果推理能力的传统观点。

Abstract: Interventional data is widely regarded as the gold standard for teaching models causal reasoning. We test this assumption in a fully controlled synthetic environment pitting observational correlation against causal effect, and find it fails instructively. In Simpson's-paradox worlds, where the two have systematically opposite signs, increasing the fraction of interventional samples in pretraining does not improve causal direction: the magnitude of the model's do()-response grows monotonically, yet its sign is copied from the observational context. What governs whether interventional evidence is used is not the training mixture but the evidence type present in the context at inference time. Under an identical training recipe, a purely observational context induces systematic sign reversal in 29/50 worlds, a mixed context in 19/50, while aligned interventional probes alone yield 41/50 correct. Erasing observational evidence from the context immediately releases the suppressed causal interpolation ability (ratio_true = +0.56); a four-state content manipulation shows the switch is content-mediated and graded. The suppression is stable across training seeds (11/11 strong reversals persist on a matched-protocol second seed) and robust as a rate at 0.93B parameters (31.8% vs. 6% reversals in the matched probe-only arm), even as absolute gains shrink four-fold. An external audit on CLadder exposes a learned positive-effect prior with a two-layer structure: sign-randomized retraining removes it in-distribution but not out-of-distribution. We summarize: the capability lives in the weights; the switch lives in the context, and activation patching localizes the switch to the middle layers' observational rows. We further quantify the sampling noise floor of probe-based causal evaluation and an evidence-averaging protocol that cuts sign errors from 26% to 9%.

</details>


### [121] [ARB: A Matched Authorship-Rewriting Benchmark Dataset for AI-Text Detector Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29539)
*Gaetano Perrone,Simon Pietro Romano*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出作者重写基准（ARB），证明传统AI文本检测器在“人类文本经LLM重写”场景下性能显著下降，但在纯LLM重写场景下保持稳健，表明传统基准无法预测此类情况。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究虽知改写会降低检测器性能，但尚不清楚在传统基准（人类vs LLM生成）上测得的性能，能否预测当人类 authored 内容被 LLM 重写时的检测行为。

Method: 构建包含1,800个源文本及四种变体（人类、直接LLM生成、LLM重写人类文本H2L、LLM重写LLM文本LLM2L）的ARB基准；评估FastDetectGPT、Binoculars-falcon-7b等五种检测器在严格1%误报率下的表现。

Result: FastDetectGPT和Binoculars-falcon-7b对直接LLM文本检出率高（91.2%-93.5%），但对LLM重写的人类文本检出率骤降60-78个百分点至15.1%-30.8%；而对同模型LLM重写的LLM文本仅轻微下降。BERT/RoBERTa防御在所有场景均低于3%召回率。

Conclusion: 传统人类vs LLM基准上的检测性能不能迁移到“人类文本经LLM重写”的场景；尽管检测器对纯LLM重写具有鲁棒性，但面对LLM修改的人类内容时性能严重受损。

Abstract: Standard AI-text detection benchmarks compare human-written text against text generated directly by large language models (LLMs). While prior work has shown that rewriting and paraphrasing can degrade detector performance, it remains unclear whether performance measured on this conventional benchmark predicts detector behavior when human-authored content is rewritten by an LLM. To address this gap, we introduce Authorship-Rewriting Benchmark (ARB), built from 1,800 human source texts (600 each from XSum, WritingPrompts, and OpenWebText) and four open-weight generators (Llama-3.2-3B, Qwen2.5-7B, Mistral-7B, Gemma-2-9B). Each source item yields four matched variants: human-written (HUMAN), direct LLM generation (Free-LLM), LLM-rewritten human text (H2L), and same-generator LLM-rewritten LLM text (LLM2L). We evaluated five detectors (FastDetectGPT, Binoculars-falcon-7b, RADAR, BERT-Defense, RoBERTa-Defense) at a strict 1%-false-positive operating point (TPR@1%FPR). FastDetectGPT and Binoculars-falcon-7b detected 91.2% and 93.5\% of direct LLM text, but only 30.8% and 15.1% of human text an LLM had rewritten, a drop of 60-78 percentage points. The same detectors retained 78.3% and 83.0% recall when LLM text was rewritten by the same model, a much smaller decline of 10-13 points. RADAR followed the same pattern (66.8% to 12.2%), while BERT-Defense and RoBERTa-Defense stayed below 3% recall across all regimes. These results show that detector performance measured on the conventional human-vs-LLM benchmark does not transfer to human-authored text revised by an LLM, even though the same detectors remain largely robust to LLM-only rewriting.

</details>


### [122] [Sycophancy Undermines Epistemic Vigilance in Cooperative Vision-Language Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29585)
*Rupak Sarkar,Neha Srikanth,Saloni Gupta,Claire Bonial,Philip Resnik,Rachel Rudinger*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出通过“找不同”对话任务评估视觉-语言模型在合作中的认知警惕性，发现模型常因迎合伙伴而忽略私有证据（即“阿谀奉承”行为）。研究证明，利用无任务特定向量引导模型可降低此类错误，使其成为更可靠的合作者。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了使AI系统成为复杂合作任务中可靠的伙伴，它们必须像人类一样具备认知警惕性，即在共享新信息时权衡私有证据与共享语境，并在出现不一致时适当指出，而非盲目附和。

Method: 构建了一个信息不对称的基于对话的“找不同”任务：两个模型分别私下查看一张图片，通过对话判断图片是否相同或找出差异。同时，使用从无关任务的阿谀奉承示例中学习到的向量对模型进行引导，以减少阿谀奉承行为。

Result: 模型在该任务中表现不佳，经常为了与对话伙伴达成一致而忽视私有图像中的关键证据，即使这种一致是不合理的。这种对认知警惕性的违背被归因为更广泛的“阿谀奉承”行为（表现为过度迁就和证据基础薄弱）。通过向量引导减少阿谀奉承后，模型减少了相关错误，成为更忠实于其证据的报告者。

Conclusion: 提升视觉-语言模型在信息不对称合作任务中的可靠性，关键在于增强其认知警惕性，抑制阿谀奉承倾向，使其能够基于私有证据做出独立判断而非盲目迎合伙伴。

Abstract: To maintain common ground in cooperative conversation, humans iteratively update their beliefs as conversation participants share new information; participants who are epistemically vigilant detect when new information conflicts with prior beliefs and take steps to repair these conflicts. In order for AI systems to serve as reliable partners in complex cooperative tasks, they must similarly weigh incoming information against their own private evidence and shared context and appropriately surface inconsistencies when they arise. To measure the epistemic vigilance of vision-language models in cooperative settings, we present an information-asymmetric, dialog-based "spot-the-difference" task. Two models are privately shown one image each, and must determine through conversation whether the images are identical or, if not, identify the difference. Models routinely fail at this: they frequently overlook key evidence in their private image in favor of agreeing with their conversational partner, even when their agreement is unwarranted. We relate these violations of epistemic vigilance to the broader behavior of sycophancy, which manifests itself in cooperative goal-oriented dialog as over-accommodation and weak evidential grounding. Our results show that model steering to reduce sycophancy with a vector learned from task-agnostic sycophancy examples can reduce epistemic vigilance-related errors, making models more faithful reporters of their evidence, and in turn, more reliable partners in information-asymmetric cooperative tasks.

</details>


### [123] [ResKV: Reconstructing Omitted Attention Contributions for Fixed-Budget KV Cache Compression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29591)
*Yuhang Zhan,Lisi Chen,Shuo Shang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出ResKV，通过结合精确主缓存和紧凑残差缓存来优化长上下文推理中的KV缓存压缩，保留被丢弃token的注意力贡献。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有缓存驱逐方法永久丢弃未选中token导致信息丢失，而基于合并的方法会扰动应保持精确的主键值；需在不牺牲精度的前提下更高效地利用固定KV预算。

Method: 将固定KV预算划分为精确主缓存和紧凑残差缓存；残差缓存重构被省略token在softmax注意力分子分母中的统计贡献；引入构建时验证代理分配残差资源，解码时动态门控调整各查询的残差贡献。

Result: 在LongBench和RULER基准测试中，多种骨干网络、缓存预算及压缩基线对比下，ResKV在保持相同保留KV预算的同时实现了广泛性能提升，并维持了压缩解码的实际效率（峰值内存和长上下文解码吞吐量）。

Conclusion: ResKV通过让主缓存token与残差条目共同参与softmax归一化，有效恢复了被忽略token的注意力质量，是一种高效且高精度的长上下文KV缓存压缩方案。

Abstract: KV cache compression is essential for efficient long-context inference. Existing eviction methods permanently discard unselected tokens and consequently remove their aggregate contribution to attention. Merging-based alternatives preserve more information but can perturb retained keys and values that should remain exact. We observe that the information omitted by cache eviction can be formulated as residual statistics in both the numerator and denominator of softmax attention. Based on this observation, we propose ResKV, which divides a fixed KV budget into an exact main cache and a compact residual cache that reconstructs the contribution of omitted tokens. ResKV lets main-cache tokens and residual entries participate in the same softmax normalization, so residual entries restore both attention numerator and denominator mass rather than acting as a post-hoc correction. A construction-time validation proxy determines residual allocation for each layer and KV head, while a decode-time dynamic gate adjusts residual contributions for individual queries. Comprehensive evaluations on LongBench and RULER, covering query-aware and query-agnostic settings, multiple backbones, cache budgets, and representative compression baselines, demonstrate broad improvements under the same retained KV budget while preserving the practical efficiency of compressed decoding, including peak memory usage and long-context decode throughput.

</details>


### [124] [FriendBench: Benchmarking Dyadic Familiarity Inference in Humans and Multimodal Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29602)
*Jeffrey M. Girard,Jason Z. Zheng,Jacqueline R. Vertino,Antony D'Avirro,Benjamin Peloquin*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出FriendBench基准，通过20秒双人破冰对话片段（文本、音频、视频），评估模型与人类在判断两人是否熟识上的表现。最佳模型准确率与人类相当，但存在“陌生人”倾向偏差；人类能从视觉行为中获益更多。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 社交情境解读不仅依赖语言，更依赖非言语行为。现有研究缺乏统一基准来量化多模态信息（特别是非言语线索）在判断人际熟悉度中的作用及模型与人类的差异。

Method: 构建FriendBench基准，包含96个平衡的双人对话样本。对比7家公司共26个模型与人类小组的表现。分析文本、音频、视频三种模态下的准确性及决策偏差，特别关注模型对“熟人”与“陌生人”类别的预测分布差异及多模态增益效果。

Result: 1. 最佳AI模型在各类模态中的准确率与人类无显著统计学差异。2. 人类预测保持平衡，而最强模型倾向于预测为“陌生人”，这反映的是先验概率差异而非歧视性偏见。3. 丰富通道（如视频）对人和模型的提升效果不同，仅人类能从语音之外的可见行为中获得额外性能增益。4. 公开了刺激材料、人类评分和模型预测数据。

Conclusion: 当前SOTA模型在识别社交熟悉度任务上已达到人类水平，但在决策策略（先验偏好）和多模态整合能力（利用视觉非言语线索）上与人类存在本质差异。该基准为后续研究提供了标准化评估框架和数据资源。

Abstract: Reading a social situation often depends on behavior, not words alone. We introduce FriendBench, a benchmark for inferring whether two people are already familiar or are meeting as strangers, from a 20-second clip of a dyadic ice-breaker conversation. Every pair answers the same type of prompt, so only the manner of interaction can reveal the answer. Across text, audio, and video, we compare 26 models from seven companies against matched human panels over 96 balanced dyads. The best model and the human crowd are statistically indistinguishable on accuracy in every modality, but reach it differently: humans stay balanced across the two answers, while the strongest models lean toward "stranger"---a difference in effective prior, not discrimination. Richer channels help both unequally, and only humans gain from visible behavior on top of speech. We release the stimuli, human ratings, and model predictions.

</details>


### [125] [Evolving language compositionality in a frequency-structured meaning space](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29642)
*Fabio De Ponte,Eloise Gaines-White,Conor Houghton,Seth Bullock*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 研究频率分布如何影响语言迭代学习中的组合性。发现高频意义可逃避语法约束，但若频率作用于小单元而非整体意义，则导致语言无法跨代传递，表明组合性依赖于学习者对形式-意义单元的习得。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究在语言迭代学习模型中，改变不同意义的出现频率（即部分意义比另一些更频繁）如何影响语言组合性的特征，以解释自然语言中高频现象与语法规则的关系。

Method: 使用迭代学习模型，通过操纵频率结构施加的对象（整体意义向量 vs. 组成部件），模拟语言在多代学习者之间的传递过程，观察其组合性和传输稳定性。

Result: 1. 当频率作用于整体意义时，高频意义能像自然语言一样逃避低频次意义所受的语法约束压力；2. 当频率作用于较小部件而非整体意义时，尽管最频繁元素被可靠习得，但语言无法跨代稳定传递。

Conclusion: 频率仅当分布在学习者能以整体方式习得的“形式-意义”单元上时，才能塑造涌现的语言结构。若频率分布于更小单元，将破坏支持组合泛化所需的 relational structure，从而阻碍语言的稳定传承。

Abstract: The iterated learning model was introduced to investigate language evolution: the way in which the characteristic properties of human languages have been shaped, at least partly, by repeated transmission from one language user to another. The key finding is that language compositionality can arise spontaneously as a consequence of language being passed repeatedly through a language learning bottleneck. Here we explore how changing the frequency of different meanings, so that some meanings occur much more frequently than others, affects the character of its compositionality. We find that, as observed in natural languages, high-frequency meanings can escape the pressure to conform to the grammar that characterizes lower-frequency meanings. However, when the frequency structure is instead imposed on parts rather than on whole meaning vectors, the language fails to transmit across generations. This occurs despite the fact that the most frequent elements are reliably learned. These results suggest that frequency can shape emergent linguistic structure only when the frequency distribution is defined over form-meaning units that learners can acquire holistically. When frequency is instead distributed over smaller units, it fails to support the relational structure required for compositional generalisation, thereby preventing stable language transmission.

</details>


### [126] [TokTier: Exact Stateful Tokenization for Agentic LLM Serving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29678)
*Zhenyu Zhang,Zhichao Cao*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出TokTier，一种状态化分词服务，解决LLM推理中因重复全量分词导致的延迟问题。通过增量修复和GPU加速，实现零分歧的分词一致性，显著降低首字生成时间（TTFT），提升吞吐量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM服务系统虽缓存KV状态，但前端每次调用仍对完整请求文本进行重新分词。对于编码代理（coding agents）频繁提交长对话历史且仅追加少量内容的场景，由于追加内容可能改变末尾的Token边界，导致缓存复用困难。数据显示，在94.1%的提示词缓存命中率下，分词操作仍占首字生成时间（TTFT）高达64%，成为性能瓶颈。

Method: TokTier是一种状态化分词服务，保证发出的Token ID与完整参考分词完全一致。其核心机制包括：1）会话延续时，仅在追加内容周围的小窗口内重新分词，并通过稳定边界检查拼接；若失败则扩大窗口或回退至全量分词。2）无复用前缀时，将GPT类正则预分词分解为本地规则，并在GPU上执行精确预分词和BPE。3）使用采样影子验证器实时检查流量以确保正确性。

Result: 在17种分词器和150亿次分裂检查中实现零分歧。增量修复速度极快（0.5-1.1ms），比HuggingFace分词快437倍，比最强缓存基线快2.1倍。GPU全量分词处理100万字符仅需0.87ms，比HuggingFace快491倍。结合vLLM，中位数TTFT降低16-34%，P99降低23%。在P99延迟低于50ms的目标下，4个修复核+1个GPU的吞吐量（1,821 req/s）是16核无状态前端的45倍以上。

Conclusion: TokTier通过高效的状态化增量分词和GPU加速，有效解决了LLM服务中分词带来的延迟瓶颈，在保证分词一致性的前提下，大幅提升了系统吞吐量和响应速度，特别适用于代理驱动的高频交互场景。

Abstract: LLM serving systems cache prompt KV state, yet most front ends still re-tokenize the full request text on every call. The cost lands on coding agents, which resubmit a long transcript after each small tool result, and reuse is hard because even a short append can change token boundaries near the end of the previous sequence. Across 153,951 calls from two agent ecosystems, the median call appends about 1.4K characters, and only 1.0-3.6% of calls start or rebuild a session with contexts of millions of characters. At a 94.1% fleet prompt-cache hit rate, tokenization reaches up to 64% of time to first token.
  TokTier is a stateful tokenization service with one contract: emitted token IDs are always identical to full reference tokenization of the request text. For a session continuation, it re-tokenizes a small window around the append and splices only after a per-request stable-boundary check, widening the window or falling back to full tokenization on failure. For a call without a reusable prefix, it decomposes GPT-family regex pre-tokenization into run-local rules and runs exact pre-tokenization and BPE on a GPU. A sampled shadow verifier re-checks live traffic.
  Across 17 tokenizer families, differential campaigns cover 1.5x10^10 split checks, a 12.4 TB real-text corpus, and 93,000+ replayed agent steps, with zero divergence. Incremental repair takes 0.5-1.1 ms from 100K to 3M characters, up to 437x faster than HF tokenization and 2.1x faster at 1M than the strongest cache-based baseline (Gigatoken) fully prewarmed. GPU full tokenization encodes a 1M-character request in 0.87 ms, up to 491x below HF and 23.4x below the fastest published CPU method. With vLLM, median time to first token drops 16-34% and P99 drops 23% under recorded bursts. Under a 50 ms P99 objective, four repair cores plus one GPU sustain 1,821 requests/s where a 16-core stateless front end saturates at 40.

</details>


<div id='cs.IR'></div>

# cs.IR [[Back]](#toc)

### [127] [RareSense: Rarity-Aware Similarity Search for Anomaly Retrieval in Transactional Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28879)
*Sidahmed Benabderrahmane,Talal Rahwan*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 提出RareSense框架，通过挖掘稀有项集和关联规则来衡量稀疏集合数据的相似度，克服了传统原子级比较（如Jaccard、余弦）受背景属性干扰的问题，在异常检测检索任务中表现优于基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统相似度度量（Jaccard、余弦、Hamming）基于原子重叠，易被频繁的背景属性主导；IDF加权虽能缓解此问题，但仍局限于原子层面，无法显式表示具有信息量的高阶共现关系。

Method: 引入RareSense框架：1) 挖掘最小稀有项集作为中间结构；2) 推导可靠的稀有关联规则；3) 将对象映射为稀疏的规则配置文件；4) 使用加权Jaccard相似度进行比较，权重结合逆支持度、置信度、提升度、结构复杂性和稳定性，使邻域由共享的稀有证据而非均匀特征重叠决定。

Result: 1) IDF加权Jaccard是RareSense的单例特例；诱导距离在原对象上为伪度量，在等价类上为度量；2) 在四个基准家族（涵盖网络安全和通用分类领域）实验中，RareSense达到最高的宏观平均查询条件检索性能；3) 统计显著性分析显示其优于原子基线；4) 在全局异常排名中表现最佳，且与多个专用检测器相当。

Conclusion: RareSense通过利用稀有高阶结构有效提升了稀疏交易异常数据的相似度搜索性能，特别是在异常共享可重复稀有高阶结构时增益最大。

Abstract: Similarity search over sparse set-valued data is often dominated by frequent background attributes because classical measures such as Jaccard, cosine, and Hamming compare objects through atomic overlap. IDF (Inverse document frequency) weighting partially reduces this effect but remains atom-wise and cannot explicitly represent informative higher-order co-occurrences. We introduce RareSense, a rarity-aware similarity framework for sparse transactional anomaly data. RareSense mines minimal rare itemsets as intermediate structures, derives reliable rare association rules, maps objects into sparse rare-rule profiles, and compares them using weighted Jaccard similarity. Rule weights combine inverse support, confidence, lift, structural complexity, and stability, so that neighborhoods are determined by shared rare evidence rather than uniform feature overlap. We show that IDF-weighted Jaccard is a restricted singleton case of RareSense, and that the induced distance is a pseudometric on the original objects and a metric over equivalence classes defined by identical rule profiles. Experiments across four benchmark families spanning cybersecurity and general categorical domains show that RareSense attains the highest observed macro-average query-conditioned retrieval performance among the evaluated similarity measures. The statistical analysis indicates significant overall differences, with corrected paired comparisons favoring RareSense over the atomic baselines. The gains remain workload-dependent and are strongest when anomalies share repeatable rare higher-order structure. For global anomaly ranking, RareSense achieves the highest observed macro-average performance while remaining statistically comparable to several strong dedicated detectors.

</details>


### [128] [LLM-Based Generative Retrieval for Snapchat Content Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28895)
*Liam Collins,Jiwen Ren,Donald Loveland,Bhuvesh Kumar,Clark Mingxuan Ju,Xuan Guo,Mo Li,Alvin Hou,Yi Cui,Peng Yang,Jian Wang,Saud Afzal Shafi,Nga Than,Ruiming Lu,Wenfeng Zhuo,Dongheng Li,Lili Zhang,Mingtao Zhang,Jinchao Ye,Vincent Xue,Chunhui Zhu,Neil Shah*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: SnapLGR是Snapchat推出的基于大语言模型（LLM）的生成式推荐系统，通过构建语义标识符（SIDs）、持续预训练和高效推理架构，解决了LLM在生成式检索中的词汇表和延迟挑战，线上A/B测试显著提升了用户观看时长和深度会话指标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将预训练LLM转化为生产环境中的生成式检索引擎面临两大挑战：一是模型需学习预训练中不存在的内部物品词汇表；二是在严格的延迟和成本约束下生成有效的物品标识符。

Method: 1. 设计语义标识符（SIDs）：从多模态物品嵌入构建，并结合基于个人页面排名（PPR）的共 engagement 对比学习，以优化码本利用率、减少冲突并注入协同信号。2. 训练策略：先进行持续预训练（CPT）以锚定新的SID token，随后对用户交互序列进行监督微调（SFT）。3. 服务优化：利用TensorRT-LLM CUDA支持的束搜索和去中心化工作循环架构实现高效部署。

Result: 在线A/B测试显示，相比现有的TIGER风格生成式检索基线，SnapLGR使观看时间（View Time）增加0.37%，使用时间（Time Spent）增加0.09%，深度会话（Deep Sessions）增加0.18%，深度会话独立用户数增加0.11%。离线分析进一步量化了模型架构、扩展性和预训练带来的增益。

Conclusion: 成功的生产级生成式检索系统需要在表示学习、词汇锚定以及高效的训练和服务方面进行联合设计。SnapLGR证明了LLM结合特定工程优化在短视频推荐场景下的有效性。

Abstract: Pretrained large language models (LLMs) are promising retrieval engines because they combine rich semantic priors, strong sequence modeling capabilities, and favorable scaling behavior. However, turning a pretrained LLM into a generative retriever in production deployment raises several challenges: the model must learn an internal item vocabulary that was absent from pretraining, and generate valid item identifiers under strict latency and cost constraints. We address these challenges through the design and launch of SnapLGR, an LLM-based generative retrieval system for short-video recommendation at Snapchat. The system is built around three main designs. First, we construct semantic identifiers (SIDs) from multimodal item embeddings and enhance them with Personalized PageRank (PPR)-based co-engagement contrastive learning, resulting in improved codebook utilization, reduced collisions, and infused collaborative signal. Second, we use continued pretraining (CPT) to ground the introduced SID tokens before supervised fine-tuning (SFT) on user interaction sequences. Third, we make SnapLGR serving practical through TensorRT-LLM CUDA-backed beam search and a decentralized worker-loop architecture. In a live A/B test, the launched system increased View Time by 0.37%, Time Spent by 0.09%, Deep Sessions by 0.18%, and Deep Sessions Unique User by 0.11% relative to the existing TIGER-style generative retrieval baseline. We then decompose this offline gap under a fixed tokenizer and quantify the gains due to model architecture, scaling, and pretraining. Overall, our deployment shows that successful production SnapLGR requires joint design across representation learning, vocabulary grounding, and efficient training and serving.

</details>


### [129] [TransX: Scaling Transformer-based Recommendation via Behavioral and Serving Stream Crossings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28940)
*Da Xu,Liyan Fang,Divya Venugopalan,Sunny Hsu,Xukai Wang,Rishav Roy Chowdhury,Cindy Liang,Nishant Satya Lakshmikanth*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: LinkedIn提出TransX架构，将推荐重构为序列到序列的动作转换问题。通过解耦长期行为与实时事件建模，并结合增量编码与KV缓存策略，在显著降低在线计算成本（约80%）的同时，实现了CTR提升6.0%和转化率提升4.4%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的基于Transformer的推荐系统通常将异构数据源（如长期用户行为和实时服务事件）合并为单一的单片令牌流。这种做法掩盖了不同数据源的因果角色和时间特征，导致建模效率低下以及高昂的训练和服务成本。

Method: 提出TransX，一种面向生产的编码器-解码器架构。该方法将推荐重新定义为序列到序列的动作转换问题，显式地将行为流建模与服务事件建模解耦。它利用可扩展的交叉注意力机制，将近线行为编码与实时服务表示相结合以预测下一个动作。此外，设计了摊销服务策略，结合增量行为编码和每请求键值（KV）缓存，使服务延迟对行为序列长度不敏感。

Result: 在LinkedIn推荐系统中的大量离线实验和大规模在线A/B测试表明：TransX consistently优于最先进的深度学习推荐模型（DLRMs）和序列基线；点击率（CTR）提升+6.0%，转化率提升+4.4%；同时，其协同设计的 serving 策略使在线计算量减少了约80%，且保持了与现有生产模型相当的服务成本。

Conclusion: TransX通过解耦异构数据建模和优化服务策略，有效解决了传统单片序列建模的效率瓶颈，在提升推荐效果的同时大幅降低了计算资源消耗，证明了其在工业级大规模推荐系统中的优越性。

Abstract: Modern industrial recommender systems (RecSys) increasingly adopt Transformer-based sequence models, with an emerging paradigm that frames recommendation as next-token prediction over a unified monolithic user sequence. However, collapsing heterogeneous data sources -- such as long-term user behaviors and real-time serving events -- into a single monolithic token stream that obscures their distinct causal roles and temporal characteristics, leading to inefficient modeling and elevated training and serving costs. We propose TransX, a production-oriented encoder-decoder architecture that reformulates recommendation as a sequence-to-sequence action transduction problem. TransX explicitly decouples behavior-stream modeling from serving-event modeling and conditions next-action decoding on scalable cross-attention between nearline behavior encodings and real-time serving representations. To enable low-latency, high-QPS deployment, TransX is co-designed with an amortized serving strategy that combines incremental behavior encoding with per-request key-value caching, rendering serving latency insensitive to behavior sequence length. Extensive offline experiments and large-scale online A/B tests on LinkedIn's recommender systems show that TransX consistently outperforms state-of-the-art DLRMs and sequential baselines, and delivers substantial CTR lift (+6.0%) and conversion gain (+4.4%) while maintaining serving costs comparable to existing production models where our co-designed serving strategy reduces online computation by approximately 80%.

</details>


### [130] [Don't Contrast the Impossible: Region-Constrained Batching for Contrastive User Modeling on a Local Community Platform](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28971)
*Seungho Han,Byeongchang Kim,Jin Yu*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 针对推荐系统中因地理限制导致的“不可能负样本”问题，提出区域约束批采样（RCBS）方法。通过构建区域同质迷你批次，用可行物品替换不可能负样本，增强对比学习信号，显著提升用户表征质量及多项业务指标，并已上线生产环境。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准对比学习假设用户可曝光任何物品（普遍曝光假设），但在如Karrot等本地社区平台中，曝光受地理位置严格限制。训练中将实际上无法展示的“不可能用户-物品对”视为负样本，稀释了对比学习信号，导致模型效果受限。

Method: 提出区域约束批采样（Region-Constrained Batch Sampling, RCBS）。该方法构建区域同质的迷你批次（mini-batches），确保用户在对比时主要面对其在现实中可能看到的物品。通过将“不可能负样本”替换为“可行负样本”，在真实曝光约束下引入更具挑战性和信息量的负样本。

Result: 离线评估和在线A/B测试表明，RCBS consistently 提升了用户表征质量，进而改善了首页Feed排序、检索以及展示广告排序的效果。生成的用户嵌入向量已在多个应用场景的生产环境中部署。

Conclusion: RCBS有效解决了地理约束下的对比学习噪声问题，证明了在局部社区场景中考虑实际曝光约束对于提升推荐系统性能的重要性，且该方法具有良好的工程落地价值。

Abstract: Contrastive learning is widely used for user modeling in large-scale recommender systems, where standard in-batch negatives implicitly assume universal exposure that any user can be shown any item. On local community platforms such as Karrot, however, exposure is geographically constrained; many user-item pairs are impossible by design yet still treated as negatives during training, diluting the contrastive learning signal. We address this impossible negatives problem and propose Region-Constrained Batch Sampling (RCBS), a simple yet effective batching method that constructs region-homogeneous mini-batches so that users are contrasted primarily against items they could feasibly see. By replacing impossible negatives with feasible ones, RCBS naturally introduces harder and more informative negatives under realistic exposure constraints. With offline evaluations and online A/B tests, we show that RCBS consistently improves user representation quality and consequently enhances home feed ranking, retrieval, and display ads ranking. The resulting user embeddings have been deployed in production across various applications.

</details>


### [131] [Think2Go: Generative Next POI Recommendation with LLM Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28997)
*Zhuang Zhuang,Shanshan Feng,Hangwei Qian,Mingqi Yang,Heng Qi,Yanming Shen,Baocai Yin*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 提出Think2Go框架，通过统一监督微调(SFT)和基于强化学习(RL)的推理，结合测试时计算扩展，解决现有POI推荐模型在捕捉深层意图和处理语义ID方面的不足。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法依赖浅层上下文信息，难以处理用户移动模式的稀疏性和复杂性；大语言模型(LLMs)因缺乏对语义ID (SIDs)的深度理解而表现不佳。

Method: 1. 构建Think2生成式POI推荐框架，统一SFT和RL-based reasoning架构，实现记忆保留与自适应推理的联合优化。
2. 采用测试时计算扩展以探索多样化的时空模式。
3. 提出两种优势加权机制进行策略校准：(1) 基于核密度估计提示认知不确定性，促进高不确定性下的探索；(2) 基于奖励归一化的优势缩放，提高训练稳定性并防止过拟合。
4. 形成隐式课程学习策略，提供细粒度、实例感知的策略更新。

Result: 增强了模型对SID表示的理解能力，提升了捕捉深层用户意图的能力，实现了更稳健的探索和更稳定的训练过程，有效防止了熵坍塌和对噪声信号的过拟合。

Conclusion: Think2Go通过结合记忆与推理、引入认知不确定性和奖励校准机制，显著提升了下一POI推荐的准确性和鲁棒性，为处理复杂用户行为模式提供了新范式。

Abstract: Next Point-of-Interest (POI) recommendation task focuses on mining user behavioral preference patterns from historical check-ins to provide personalized suggestions for the next destination. Existing methods primarily rely on shallow contextual information and handcrafted feature interactions to predict the next POI. However, the inherent sparsity and complexity of user mobility patterns limit the computational capacity of non-reasoning models to capture deep intent, while large language models (LLMs) perform suboptimally because they lack a deep understanding of semantic IDs (SIDs) when SIDs are trained separately. To address these limitations, we propose Think2Go, a novel generative next POI recommendation framework, which enhances the model's comprehension of SID representations and explores diverse spatial-temporal patterns via test-time computational scaling. We unify supervised fine-tuning (SFT) and reinforcement learning (RL)-based reasoning within a single architecture, enabling joint optimization of memorization and adaptive reasoning to better retain user behavior patterns while exploring diverse user preferences. To further calibrate policy optimization in adaptive reasoning, we propose two advantage weighting mechanisms that integrate (1) prompt epistemic uncertainty, estimated via kernel density methods to assess the spatial-temporal periodic pattern alignment between queries and user history, promoting increased exploration under high epistemic uncertainty; and (2) reward-informed advantage scaling, captured by normalizing rewards against their maxima to adapt update magnitudes, thereby improving training stability and mitigating overfitting to noisy signals. This joint calibration forms an implicit curriculum learning strategy, delivering fine-grained, instance-aware policy updates that prevent entropy collapse and support robust exploration.

</details>


### [132] [PaletteID: Prototype-Composed Semantic Identifiers for Multimodal CTR Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29000)
*Huanyu Liu,Baining Chen,Hui Liu,Zengyang Li,Ziyi Huang*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 提出PaletteID (PID)，一种基于原型的语义标识符，通过原型调色板解决现有离散化方法丢失细粒度信号和层级依赖问题，显著提升CTR预测精度及长尾物品表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有将多模态嵌入离散化为语义标识符（SID）的方法存在两大局限：1. 码本分配无法保留语义相关性且丢弃原始嵌入空间中的细粒度连续信号；2. 残差码路径高度依赖前缀码，限制了层级标识符的有效表示扩展性。

Method: 提出PaletteID (PID)：1. 使用语义质量感知确定性点过程（SQ-DPP）构建原型调色板，兼顾局部内容密度和全局语义多样性以选择代表性原型项目作为锚点；2. 为目标物品检索语义相关的原型序列并聚合为信息丰富的PID表示，实现丰富且互补的语义建模。

Result: 在两个公开数据集上的实验表明，PID一致性地提高了点击率（CTR）预测准确率，对长尾物品的增益更大；相比现有的残差SID方法，PID产生了更鲁棒的标识符分配和更具可解释性的token语义。

Conclusion: PaletteID通过基于原型的语义标识符机制，有效克服了传统离散化方法的缺陷，在多模态推荐系统中实现了更优的CTR预测性能和更强的可解释性。

Abstract: Multimodal information can improve the accuracy of click-through rate (CTR) prediction and effectively alleviate item cold-start and long-tail problems. Recent studies commonly discretize pretrained multimodal embeddings into semantic identifiers (SIDs), allowing the model to learn task-specific semantic representations for recommendation. However, existing methods still provide limited gains due to two major limitations. First, codebook assignment fails to preserve semantic relevance and discards fine-grained continuous signals in the original embedding space. Second, the residual code paths are highly dependent on prefix codes, which limits the effective representational scalability of hierarchical identifiers. To address these issues, we propose PaletteID (PID), a prototype-based semantic identifier. Inspired by palette-based color composition, PID uses a compact set of representative prototype items as semantic anchors to bridge pretrained multimodal content space and recommendation models. Specifically, we first construct a prototype palette with Semantic Quality-Aware Determinantal Point Process (SQ-DPP), which jointly considers local content density and global semantic diversity. Then, for each target item, PID retrieves a sequence of semantically related prototypes and aggregates them into an informative PID representation, enabling rich and complementary semantic modeling. Extensive experiments on two public datasets demonstrate that PID consistently improves CTR prediction and yields larger gains for long-tail items. PID also produces more robust identifier assignments and provides more interpretable token semantics than existing residual SID methods.

</details>


### [133] [EvoReason: Self-Evolving Reasoning Primitive-Guided On-Policy Distillation for Latent Reasoning in Generative Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29010)
*Zhuang Zhuang,Zhipeng Wei,Rongfeng Guo,Shijie Li,Peng Zhao,Jie Chen,Fei Pan*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 提出EvoReason框架，通过原始引导的在线策略蒸馏和自进化机制，解决推荐系统中潜在推理因原始思维链冗余和不稳定导致的迁移效果差问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有潜在推理方法直接蒸馏原始思维链（CoT），但推荐推理轨迹存在表达冗余和路径不稳定，导致监督信号次优，难以学习可迁移的潜在推理表示。

Method: 提出EvoReason自进化潜在推理框架：1.从高质量代理推荐轨迹中提取可复用的推理原语作为伪工具；2.赋予教师模型基于原语的感知推理能力，生成结构化、低冗余的CoT监督信号；3.引入自进化在线策略蒸馏机制，根据学生模型的潜在推理结果动态优化推理过程，实现闭环协同进化。

Result: 通过闭环协同进化，持续改进潜在推理行为，使CoT监督与潜在空间更好对齐，从而实现更有效的推理知识迁移。

Conclusion: EvoReason通过自适应对齐显式推理监督与学生潜在空间，有效解决了推荐场景中推理轨迹的不稳定性问题，提升了潜在推理的效率和准确性。

Abstract: Generative recommendation benefits from reasoning-enhanced inference, and latent reasoning offers an efficient paradigm by encoding intermediate reasoning processes into compact continuous representations for latency-sensitive deployment. Despite its efficiency, existing latent reasoning approaches typically rely on directly distilling raw chain-of-thought (CoT) trajectories into latent representations, assuming that textual reasoning traces provide sufficient supervision. However, recommendation reasoning trajectories contain diverse reasoning processes with redundant expressions and unstable reasoning paths, making raw CoT supervision suboptimal for learning transferable latent reasoning representations. To address this challenge, we propose EvoReason, a self-evolving latent reasoning framework that adaptively aligns explicit reasoning supervision with the student's latent reasoning space through primitive-guided on-policy distillation. First, EvoReason extracts reusable reasoning primitives from high-quality agentic recommendation trajectories, where each primitive captures an essential reasoning behavior and serves as a pseudo-tool for structured teacher reasoning. Then, based on these primitives, we equip the teacher with primitive-aware reasoning capabilities, enabling it to generate structured CoT supervision with reduced redundancy and improved consistency. Finally, during latent reasoning optimization, EvoReason introduces a self-evolving on-policy distillation mechanism, where the primitive-guided reasoning process evolves according to the student's latent reasoning outcomes. Through this closed-loop co-evolution, policy updates continuously improve latent reasoning behaviors is refined according to the resulting latent reasoning outcomes, enabling progressively better-aligned CoT supervision and more effective reasoning transfer.

</details>


### [134] [Reproducing LightMem: Naive RAG Is Just as Good for Memory Management](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29104)
*Yongjie Zhou,Shuai Wang,Bevan Koopman,Guido Zuccon*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 研究复现并对比了轻量级记忆管理方法LightMem与朴素RAG（直接从原始对话轮次检索）。发现LightMem的优势并非普遍存在，其性能高度依赖检索器选择，且构建的记忆会丢失部分相关信息。在匹配检索深度时，Naive RAG通常表现更好；LightMem仅在回答token预算紧张时有优势。结论是LightMem提供的是上下文效率权衡而非通用优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长期对话代理需要访问早期交互信息，但重复提供完整历史成本高。LightMem作为一种轻量级记忆管理方法声称具有高效性，但其对检索器的敏感性以及记忆构建是否丢弃关键信息尚不明确，缺乏与其他基线方法的全面对比。

Method: 1. 复现LightMem方法；2. 将其与Naive RAG（直接从原始用户轮次检索）进行对比实验；3. 评估不同检索器对LightMem存储库性能的影响；4. 在不同token预算和检索深度下比较两种方法的答案准确率；5. 通过Oracle评估分析记忆构建过程中信息丢失情况。

Result: 1. 检索器选择是性能变化的主要来源：固定LightMem存储库时，仅更换检索器即可使答案准确率从58.1%波动至75.5%。2. 构建的记忆并未始终优于原始轮次检索。3. 在匹配的检索深度下，Naive RAG通常表现更佳。4. LightMem主要在严格的回答token预算限制下表现更好。5. Oracle评估证实记忆构建过程确实移除了部分与答案相关的信息。

Conclusion: LightMem提供的是一种上下文效率的权衡（trade-off），而非相对于Naive RAG的通用优势。其价值取决于具体的检索器选择和可用的token预算。未来工作应关注检索、重排序、查询构建及其与原始和构建型记忆表示之间的相互作用。

Abstract: Long-term conversational agents require access to information from earlier interactions, such as a user's preferences, past requests, or previously mentioned facts. Repeatedly providing the full dialogue history can be expensive as conversations grow, so many memory approaches instead transform past interactions into compact entries that can be retrieved when needed. LightMem is a recent lightweight memory-management approach that reports strong effectiveness while maintaining relatively low construction cost. However, it still relies on a separate constructed memory representation and is evaluated with only one retriever, leaving unclear how sensitive its results are to retriever choice and whether memory construction discards answer-relevant information.
  In this study, we reproduce LightMem and compare it with Naive RAG, which retrieves directly from raw user turns. We recover LightMem's main configuration trend, but find that retriever choice is a major source of performance variation: changing only the retriever over a fixed LightMem store shifts answer accuracy from 58.1% to 75.5%. Constructed memories also do not consistently outperform raw-turn retrieval. Naive RAG generally performs better at matched retrieval depths, whereas LightMem performs better mainly under tight answering-token budgets. Oracle evaluation further shows that memory construction removes some answer-relevant information.
  Overall, LightMem offers a context-efficiency trade-off rather than a general advantage over Naive RAG. Its value depends on the retriever and available token budget, motivating future work on retrieval, reranking, query formulation, and their interaction with raw and constructed memory representations.

</details>


### [135] [GALA: Generative Aligned Learning for Adaptive Multimodal Representation in the Taobao Shangou Recommender System](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29213)
*Jiping Liu,Zhongmin Zhang,Zisen Sang,Zhijia Fang,Tao Ouyang,Ma Jiang,Shaopeng Liang,Zeyang Hou,Guodong Cao,Jia Jia*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: GALA 是一种用于外卖推荐系统的三阶段多模态融合框架，通过引入“生成式强化学习对齐”中间阶段，解决了图像-文本语义预训练与用户行为排名模型之间的对齐问题。该方法在淘宝闪购上线，服务超2亿日活用户，在线A/B测试显示订单量提升0.55%，离线指标AUC提升0.12-0.20。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代外卖推荐系统利用多模态信号（图像、文本、交互历史）提升体验，但异构模态的有效融合仍具挑战。主流两阶段方法中，基于内容的图像-文本编码器预训练与基于行为的排序模型分离，导致语义理解与用户行为模式之间缺乏对齐，难以适应动态变化的用户意图。

Method: 提出 GALA 三阶段流水线：1) 行为感知的三元组预训练：利用搜索日志中的查询-图像-文本对进行早期用户意图捕捉；2) 生成式RL对齐中间阶段：通过基于转换奖励的优化（GRPO）精炼多模态嵌入，弥合预训练与微调差距，实现与下游目标的动态对齐；3) 自适应门控融合：将多模态嵌入与ID嵌入结合，使用混合损失函数，在长期ID主导训练中保留多模态贡献。

Result: GALA 已在淘宝闪购生产环境部署，服务超过2亿日活跃用户。相比最先进（SOTA）方法，离线评估取得 +0.12/+0.20 AUC 的稳定增益及更优的 PCOC 指标。大规模在线 A/B 测试表明订单量增加 0.55%。

Conclusion: GALA 通过创新的三阶段流程和生成式强化学习对齐机制，有效解决了多模态推荐系统中的语义-行为对齐难题。其在工业级规模上的成功部署和显著的在线性能提升，验证了该方法在不同需求模式下的鲁棒性和有效性。

Abstract: Modern recommender systems in food delivery increasingly leverage multimodal signals, including images, text, and user interaction histories, to enhance user experience, yet effective fusion of these heterogeneous modalities remains challenging, hindering both the joint modeling of multimodal signals and adaptation to evolving user intent. In mainstream two-stage approaches, the separation between content-semantic pretraining of image-text encoders and behavior-driven ranking models limits alignment between semantic understanding and user behavior patterns. To address these issues, we present GALA, a three-stage pipeline whose core innovation lies in an intermediate "generative RL alignment" stage that constructs multimodal pretraining data from user behavior and refines it via conversion-based rewards, effectively bridging the pretraining-fine-tuning gap to align with downstream objectives. GALA comprises three stages: first, behavior-aware triplet pretraining on query-image-text pairs from search logs to early capture user intent and content preferences; second, a novel intermediate stage that refines multimodal embeddings through reward-driven optimization (GRPO) to dynamically align them with user behavior and bridge the pretraining-fine-tuning gap; and finally, integration of multimodal and ID embeddings via adaptive gating with a hybrid loss, preserving multimodal contributions under long-term ID-dominant training. GALA has been deployed in the production environment at Taobao Shangou, serving over 200 million daily active users. Compared with state-of-the-art (SOTA) methods, it delivers consistent offline gains of +0.12/+0.20 AUC along with better PCOC metrics. Large-scale online A/B tests further report a 0.55 percent increase in order volume, confirming GALA's effectiveness at industrial scale and its robustness across diverse demand patterns.

</details>


### [136] [RecHarness: A Bandit-Routed Agentic Harness for Self-Evolving Recommender Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29241)
*Haoran Ling,Yuecheng Li,Zeyu Song,Jing Yao,Shuwen Kang,Chi Lu,Wenjin Wu,Peng Jiang*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 提出RecHarness，一种基于Bandit路由的智能体框架，用于自动化推荐模型优化。通过将搜索方向选择（Bandit路由器）与具体假设生成（LLM）分离，并结合“跳跃盆地”机制克服局部停滞，在有限实验预算下实现了比纯LLM推理搜索更稳定、高效的性能提升，并在实际在线A/B测试中显著提升了广告收益等核心指标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代推荐模型的优化严重依赖工程师手动迭代架构、目标和训练策略。虽然基于大语言模型（LLM）的智能体可以自动化这一试错过程，但在有限的实验预算下，让LLM同时选择修改方向和生成具体假设往往导致搜索不稳定且效率低下。

Method: 提出了RecHarness（Bandit-Routed Agentic Harness），将优化过程分为两步：1）使用Bandit路由器根据历史验证反馈选择下一个修改方向；2）LLM在选定的方向内生成具体的优化假设和可执行的代码编辑。此外，引入“跳跃盆地”（jump-basin）机制，当局部编辑陷入停滞时激活结构跳跃臂，以维持长期探索能力。

Result: 在多个推荐任务、数据集和模型骨干网络上，RecHarness相比LLM推理搜索实现了更稳定的性能提升，并更有效地利用了有限的试验预算。在大规模短视频广告平台的7天在线A/B测试中，该候选方案使ADVV提升2.084%，收入提升0.534%，曝光量提升0.559%。

Conclusion: RecHarness通过解耦搜索方向选择与具体假设生成，并结合有效的长期探索机制，成功解决了LLM在推荐模型自动化优化中搜索不稳定的问题，证明了其在工业级大规模场景下的有效性和实用性。

Abstract: Optimizing modern recommender models still depends heavily on engineers manually iterating over architectural, objective, and training-strategy changes. While LLM-based agents can automate this trial-and-error process, allowing the LLM to both select modification directions and generate concrete hypotheses often leads to unstable search under limited experiment budgets. Inspired by the above challenge, we propose RecHarness, a Bandit-Routed Agentic Harness for automated recommender model optimization. RecHarness separates the optimization process into two steps: a bandit router selects the next modification direction according to historical validation feedback, while the LLM generates a concrete optimization hypothesis and executable code edit within the selected direction. To sustain long-horizon exploration, RecHarness uses a jump-basin mechanism to activate a structural-jump arm when local edits stagnate. Across multiple recommendation tasks, datasets, and model backbones, RecHarness achieves more stable performance improvements and uses limited trial budgets more effectively than LLM-reasoning search. During a 7-day online A/B test on a large-scale short-video advertising platform, the selected candidate improves ADVV by 2.084%, Revenue by 0.534%, and Exposure by 0.559%. Code is available at https://github.com/6lyc/RecHarness.

</details>


### [137] [Language Models Agree With Each Other, Not With Readers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29274)
*Kazuki Nakayashiki,Keisuke Watanabe*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本研究挑战了“语言模型趋同”的观点，通过对比人类读者自然标记行为发现，尽管模型间存在高度一致性，但这种一致性并未超过人类读者之间的自然重叠水平，且该现象并非由确定性或提示词导致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究在衡量语言模型同质化时，通常依赖专门设计的人类判断数据，这可能导致人为偏差。本研究旨在通过引入非为特定目的构建的自然人类参考数据，更准确地评估模型与人类在阅读标记行为上的一致性差异。

Method: 使用2,523个来自120份网络文档的人类读者标记集作为基准，这些标记是用户在默认不显示他人标记的平台上出于个人原因生成的。计算重合度时，采用大小匹配的句集重叠减去基于深度和长度带重采样预期的重叠值，并验证了随机基线的校准效果。测试涵盖18个模型臂、11家供应商及不同权重配置。

Result: 中位数结果显示，模型对之间的一致性（+0.093）高于人类基准（+0.040），99个模型配对完全超出人类区间。前沿模型表现尤为显著（+0.0203），是自重复调用一致性的两倍。然而，最小规模模型的一致性处于人类水平。模型并未比读者表现出可检测的更高一致性，且在同等深度和长度下，表面特征无法区分其选择。

Conclusion: 模型间的高一致性并非源于确定性、提示词或程序，而是具有分级特性。尽管调整过程可缩小差距但无法消除，且新发布模型在样本外测试中仍未超越人类区间。结论指出，“多个模型的集合并不等同于多个独立群体”，暗示模型在统计分布上仍缺乏真正的多样性，但其趋同程度需相对于自然人类行为重新审视。

Abstract: Claims that language models homogenise are usually measured against human judgements collected for the study, which makes the human side an artifact of the design: a crowdworker given the model's instruction is running the model's prompt. We measure convergence against a human reference nobody built for the purpose -- 2,523 reader mark sets across 120 web documents, produced by people highlighting for their own reasons on a platform where the overlay of others' marks is off by default.
  Agreement is the overlap between two size-matched sentence sets minus the overlap expected when each is resampled within its own depth-and-length bands. The null's calibration is demonstrated, not asserted: every pair involving a random baseline lands within 0.006 of zero. On the median document each party names 14 sentences of 70; two readers share 4.1 and two models 8.7.
  Across 18 model arms spanning 11 vendors, 3 countries and both weight regimes, the median of 153 model pairs is +0.093 against a human yardstick of +0.040, and 99 sit entirely above the human interval. Two frontier models from rival labs reach +0.203, twice what GPT-4o agrees with itself on a second call. The effect is not determinism, prompt wording, procedure, vendor or routing, and it is graded: the smallest models agree at the human level. No model agrees with readers detectably more than a reader does, and at equal depth and length no surface feature separates their choices.
  The multiples are procedure-dependent and the ordering is not: models are cut to their sharpest set while a reader's is a random draw from what they marked, and blunting the models alike halves the gap without closing it. Tested out of sample on four models released after this analysis, against predictions fixed beforehand, none clears the human interval. A population simulated from several models is not several populations.

</details>


### [138] [Bridging the Question-Answer Gap in Retrieval-Augmented Generation: Hypothetical Prompt Embeddings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29402)
*Domen Vake,Jernej Vičič,Aleksandar Tošić*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 提出HyPE框架，将假设性内容生成从查询时移至索引阶段，通过预计算假设提示并嵌入数据块，实现无延迟的检索增强生成，显著提升检索精度和召回率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决RAG系统中用户查询与文档文本之间的风格差距问题，克服现有运行时解决方案（如HyDE）引入额外计算开销和延迟的缺陷。

Method: 提出假设性提示嵌入（HyPE），在索引阶段为每个数据块预计算多个假设性提示，并将数据块嵌入替代提示，将检索转化为“问题-问题”匹配任务，避免查询时的合成答案生成。

Result: 在六个常见数据集上的实验表明，相比标准方法，HyPE可将检索上下文精度提高高达42个百分点，召回率提高高达45个百分点，且兼容重排序、多向量检索等RAG进阶技术。

Conclusion: HyPE有效解决了风格差距问题，在不增加查询延迟的前提下显著提升了检索性能，为RAG系统提供了一种高效且可扩展的优化方案。

Abstract: Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) systems synergize retrieval mechanisms with generative language models to enhance the accuracy and relevance of responses.
